Let's design a robust deterministic optimizer:
```
def optimize_peak(a, deadline, lr=0.05, topk=20, beta_mom=0.9):
    p = make_prob(a)
    score = fast_score(p)
    # Adam moments for d
    m = zeros; v=zeros
    iter=0
    while time<deadline:
        q=conv(p,p)
        maxq=q.max(); score=2n maxq
        # weights over top peaks: select indices q> quantile
        # robust: top = sort q; weights = softmax((q-topqtail)/temp)
        top_indices = np.argpartition(q, -topk)[-topk:]
        w = np.zeros(2n-1)
        vals=q[top_indices]
        temp = max((vals[0]-vals[-1])*0.5, maxq*0.01, 1e-12)
        weights = np.exp((vals - vals[0])/temp); weights/=weights.sum()
        # gradient d = 2*corr(p, coeff_weights)
        d = gradient_conv(p, weights)
        d = d - d.mean() # sum fixed first-order
        # Adam
        m = beta1*m+(1-beta1)*d
        v = beta2*v+(1-beta2)*d*d
        step = lr * m/(sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
        # limit step magnitude to avoid blowup
        step = np.clip(step, -2, 2)
        # multiplicative candidate
        cand = p * np.exp(-step)
        cand = cand / cand.sum()
        # Also try a small projected Euclidean step: cand2 = p - eta*d; clip normalize
        # evaluate fast_score candidate; accept if < current* (1-1e-12)
        # If no accept, halve lr.
    return p
```
But using `np.exp(-step)` with step maybe large; clip. Need ensure cand positive; exp of negative large underflow ok, but zeros maybe. We can use `np.exp(np.clip(-step, -50, 50))`.

Gradient of weighted peaks: weights length L=2n-1. Need d_i = 2 Σ_{j=0}^{n-1} p_j w_{i+j}. We can compute via convolution of p and reversed w. Let `w_rev = w[::-1]`, length L. conv = ifft(fft(p, N)*fft(w_rev,N)).real. d = conv[n-1 : n-1+n] ? Let's index. conv[t]=Σ_j p_j w_rev[t-j]=Σ_j p_j w[L-1 - (t-j)] =Σ_j p_j w[L-1 - t + j]. def get_parent_sequence():
```
    try:
       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except NameError:
       p=None
    if p is None or p.ndim!=1 or p.size==0: return None
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    if p.sum()<=0: return None
    return p
```

```
Local search function:
```
def local_search(p, budget, best_score, rng):
    p = np.maximum(p,0); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: p=ones; p/=p.sum()  # sum=1.
    n=len(p)
    # scale internally to 1; fast_score returns 2n max(p*p).
    cur = fast_score(p)
    best = p.copy(); best_fast=cur
    # Try using actual p scaled to max500? Only for actual eval.
    last_actual = -inf
    # Adam moments
    m = zeros(n); v=zeros(n)
    beta1=0.7; beta2=0.95? For mirror steps, maybe simpler.
    lr = 0.2
    no_improve=0
    smooth_frac = 0.2
    iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<budget:
        q = fft_conv(p,p)
        maxq = q.max(); cur=2*n*maxq
        if cur < best_fast - 1e-10: best=...
        # weights
        temp = smooth_frac*maxq + 1e-15
        w = np.exp((q - maxq)/temp); w.sum()
        d = gradient(p,w)
        d -= d.mean()
        # normalize by rms
        rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(d*d))
        if rms>0: d/=rms
        # Adam? Since d normalized, maybe direct eta. Use momentum m = 0.9m+0.1d
        m=0.8*m+0.2*d
        # steps schedule
        accepted=False
        for eta in [0.05,0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
            cand = p * np.exp(-eta*m)
            cand = np.maximum(cand,0)
            cs=cand.sum(); if cs<=0: continue
            cand/=cs
            cscore=fast_score(cand)
            if cscore < cur - 1e-10:
                p=cand; cur=cscore; accepted=True; break
        if not accepted:
            # try Euclidean projected step
            for eta in [0.01,0.02,0.05]:
                cand = p - eta*(d - d.mean())
                # project to simplex: clip negative to0, normalize (not exact but okay)
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0); cs=cand.sum(); if cs>0 cand/=cs
                cscore=fast_score(cand)
                if cscore < cur - 1e-10: accept
            if not accept: no_improve +=1; lr?; if no_improve > 50: perturb; smooth_frac *=0.7? or *=1.3
        else: no_improve=0; maybe smooth_frac = min(0.5, smooth_frac*1.05) (focus peaks) or reduce?
        # periodic entropy/temperature: every 100 iters random restart? 
        # every 500 evaluate actual scaled to max500
        if iter%200==0:
            p_list = scale_to_500(p)
            acts=actual_score(p_list)
            if acts < best_actual_global? (through nonlocal? use return best with score)
        iter++
    return best_p, best_actual? 
```

Coordinate ascent for fixed M:
Input a nonnegative with max c≤M. Repeatedly for i=0..n-1 set a_i to max feasible given others (computed via min bounds) maybe with damping. Sum nondecreasing. Continue until no improvement. This directly optimizes S for fixed M. It doesn't require gradient/FFT; O(n^2) per pass. For n=5582, one pass O15M vector ops? We can implement with direct numpy per coord; maybe fine for few passes. It may escape baseline? Starting from parent scaled to M, it can increase coords; but if a coordinate's current bound is tight, it cannot increase. Some may. It cannot decrease, so if current shape has too high early variables preventing others, it may local max. But we can combine with random decreases/perturbations: after coordinate passes saturate, reduce some coordinates proportionally (or random) to create slack, then coordinate ascend again. This is like cyclic coordinate with annealing. Could be very effective for hard target.

Let's detail coordinate fixed-M algorithm (maximize sum):
Given a (scale such that max≤M), S=sum.
For each coordinate i:
  x=a_i
  compute max feasible y as above using current c and a. If y < x - tol (due numerical), set y=x (or min? maintain feasible). If y>x*(1+tol): update a_i = new_y (maybe damped: x + (y-x)*0.5) and c update.
To allow moving beyond local max, we can periodically apply a "decay" to selected coordinates not at lower bound: a = α a * weight? But must keep max≤M; decreasing is always feasible (all c decrease). So we can decrease high-sensitivity coordinates, then coordinate ascent will refill. A cycle: coordinate ascend to saturation, then multiply a by (1 - δ * v) where v is subgradient (peak sensitivity) to shift weight from peaks; scale? Decreasing maintains feasibility; then refill. This is analogous to stochastic approximation.

This could be more stable and directly aims at target. We can implement for target M slightly above theoretical to ensure feasible. Starting from any feasible shape. Coordinate ascent finds max S for M. We can vary M lower? Increasing M lowers S? Since scaling to M: shape optimum S_M maybe proportional √M? Actually problem homogeneous: If a feasible for M, scale by α gives max α^2 M, sum αS. Define objective max S( M). Since S( M) ∝√M? For fixed shape, scaling M changes S by √M. Let's design coordinate ascent in probability-like scale fixed M=1. Since scaling invariant, set M=1 for optimization. Then objective score=2n/S^2. We want maximize S subject max c≤1. This is shape optimization independent of n scale. For each candidate shape, scale to max=1: `a = a / sqrt(max(c))` (since max scales square). Then S = sum(a). Score =2n/S^2. Coordinate ascent for M=1 from this a.

Computing max feasible y for coordinate i with current c (vector length L) and a:
We can compute using vectorized ops. For each i:
- valid offset j=0..n-1 corresponds k=i+j.
- `a_j = a` (all j)
- c_seg = c[i:i+n]
- `is_self = (j == i)`; but k=i+j; self when i+j=2i => j=i.
- For j≠i: base = c_seg - 2*x*a_j
- For j=i: base = c_seg - x*x
- bounds:
   if j==i: b = sqrt(max(0, M - base))
   else: if a_j>0: b=(M-base)/(2*a_j) else inf
- However, using c_seg current includes contributions of x. For j such that a_j=0 and not self, bound inf.
- y = min(b). Since M=1, y should be finite due self if 2i within range (always), bound sqrt(M - base_self) ≤ sqrt(M) ≤1. So y≤1. Good.
But computing for all j each i: `base = c[i:i+n] - 2*x*a`; then set base[i] = c[2*i]-x*x. Then `bounds = np.full(n, np.inf); np.divide(M-base, 2*a, out=bounds, where=a>0); bounds[i]=sqrt...; y=np.min(bounds)`. Need avoid division by zero and negative base. Use `np.maximum(M-base,0)` for self/linear? If M-base negative due infeasibility, bound 0. For linear, if base > M, current infeasible; can set y=0? But should not if feasible. numerical.
- Damp: if y < x*(1-tol), set y=x (or maybe x= min? to maintain feasibility). If y > x*(1+tol), set new_y = x + (y-x)*step (step e.g. 0.5) to avoid oscillation. Update c:
  delta = new_y - x
  For k=i..i+n-1: if k==2i: c[k] += new_y^2 - x^2 else c[k] += 2*delta*a[j] where j=k-i.
  But when k==2i (j=i), the formula 2*delta*a[i] not correct. So update segment: `c[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a`; then adjust self: `c[2*i] -= 2*delta*a[i]; c[2*i] += new_y*new_y - x*x`. Because we added 2δa_i erroneously; correct self increment is y^2-x^2. Set a[i]=new_y. Also maintain S: S += delta.
After each coordinate, ensure no c >M+tol? Could accumulate numeric; periodically recompute c via FFT/direct from a to clean.

Complexity: For n=5582, each i vector length n => 31M operations per pass, plus Python loop 5582. With numpy per i, each creates arrays; maybe ~0.5-1s per pass. Many passes 100 =>100s. Could be okay. But memory allocations 5582*? heavy. We can optimize by using preallocated `seg`? Simpler first.

Let's implement fast_score for internal:
```
def fast_score_array(a):
    n=len(a)
    if n==0: return inf
    s = a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return inf
    # scale to sum=1 for score: score independent; q = (a/s)*(a/s) conv *? Actually max q = max(a*a)/S^2. score=2n*max(a*a)/S^2.
    # Compute conv of normalized p to avoid overflow.
    p = a/s
    out = fft_conv(p,p)
    maxb = max(out)
    if maxb<0 and maxb>-1e-12: maxb=0
    return 2*n*maxb
```
But if we want fast_score with sum fixed not necessary; use normalized to 1. Good.

`fft_conv(a,b)` top-level:
```
def fft_conv(a,b):
    nout=len(a)+len(b)-1
    n=1 << (nout-1).bit_length()
    ifHAVE_SCIPSY:
       fa=fftr(a,n); fb=fftr(b,n)
       return irfft(fa*fb,n)[:nout]
    else:
       fa=fft(a,n); fb=fft(b,n)
       return ifft(fa*fb,n).real[:nout]
```
For complex? a real. Use `scipy.fft.rfft` faster. Import:
```
try:
  from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
  _HAS_SCIPY=True
except Exception:
  from numpy.fft import fft, ifft
```
Need top-level no closures. Fine.

`gradient_weights(p,w)`:
```
def corr_p_weights(p,w):
    n=len(p); L=len(w); N=1 << (2*n + L -2? Need convolution length n+L-1 = 3n-2. Next pow2.
    if scipy: a=rfft(p,N); b=rfft(w[::-1],N); conv=irfft(a*b,N)[:n+L-1]
    else: ...
    idx = (L-1) - np.arange(n)  # 2n-2 - i
    return conv[idx]
```
Check N: convolution length len(p)+len(w_rev)-1 = n+L-1 = 3n-2. Next pow2. For n=5582, 16744 ->32768. Good. q conv length 2n-1 ->16384. separate.

Could reuse FFT of p for q and gradient? Gradient needs FFT of p multiplied with FFT(w_rev). We can compute `Pf = rfft(p, Nq)` for q with Nq, but gradient needs larger N; can't reuse. Could compute q via same N as gradient (N=32768) to reuse Pf: compute Nbig = nextpow2(3n-2). Compute Pf = rfft(p,Nbig); q = irfft(Pf*Pf,Nbig)[:2n-1]; then grad: w_rev_fft = rfft(w_rev,Nbig); conv=irfft(Pf*Wf,Nbig). This uses one FFT of p plus one of w. Good. Local search details:
```
def local_search_peak(p, deadline, max_actual_evals=30):
    n=len(p); p=p.copy(); p=np.maximum(p,0); p=sum...
    if p.sum()==0: p=ones
    cur=fast_score(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_fast=cur
    # actual initial
    scale=scale_for_eval(p); actual=actual_score(scale); best_actual_p=scale.copy(); best_actual=actual
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    no=0; smooth=0.25; eval_counter=0
    lr=0.1
    while time < deadline:
       # compute q and weights
       q=fft_conv(p,p); maxq=q.max(); cur=2*n*maxq
       if cur < best_fast - 1e-10: best_fast=cur; best_p=p.copy()
       temp = smooth*maxq + 1e-18
       z=(q-maxq)/temp
       # clip z min -50 to avoid underflow issues? underflow okay; exp(-1000)=0
       w=np.exp(np.clip(z, -50, 0)); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum<=0: w[np.argmax(q)]=1 else w/=wsum
       grad=2*corr_p_weights(p,w)
       grad = grad - grad.mean()
       rms=np.sqrt(np.mean(grad*grad))
       if rms>0: grad=grad/rms
       # momentum
       m=0.8*m+0.2*grad
       # normalize m
       rm=np.sqrt(np.mean(m*m)); if rm>0: m=m/rm
       accepted=False
       for eta in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5]:
           cand = p * np.exp(-eta*m)
           cand = np.maximum(cand, 0)
           cs=cand.sum()
           if cs<=0: continue
           cand = cand/cs
           cscore=fast_score(cand)
           if cscore < cur - 1e-11:
               p=cand; cur=cscore; accepted=True; no=0; break
       if not accepted:
           # try Euclidean
           for eta in [0.005,0.01,0.02,0.05]:
               cand = p - eta*m
               cand = np.maximum(cand,0); cs=cand.sum(); if cs<=0: continue
               cand=cand/cs
               cscore=fast_score(cand)
               if cscore < cur -1e-11:
                   accept
           if not accepted:
               no +=1
               # adjust smooth: if no>20, decrease smooth (focus) or increase? If stuck, perturb.
               if no > 10:
                   smooth *= 0.5
                   if smooth < 0.01: smooth=0.01
               if no > 30:
                   # random multiplicative perturbation
                   eps=0.05
                   p *= 1.0 + eps*np.random.standard_normal(n)
                   p=np.maximum(p,0); p/=p.sum()
                   m*=0.5
                   no=0
       else:
           # slowly focus peaks
           smooth = min(0.5, smooth*1.02)
       # periodic actual eval
       if eval_counter % 500 == 0:
           ev=scale_for_eval(p); av=actual_score(ev)
           if av < best_actual: best_actual=av; best_actual_p=ev.copy()
           eval_counter +=1 maybe track actual evals; if maxed skip.
       # Also update best_actual if fast best? no
    final ev=scale_for_eval(p); av=actual_score(ev); if av<best_actual...
    # Also return best_p scaled? We'll return best_actual_p and best_actual, and best_fast_p
    return best_actual_p, best_actual, best_p, best_fast
```

Implementing coordinate ascent with decays may be more effective than gradient for fixed M. Let's combine:
- Work with a scaled to max=1 (M=1). Maintain c = convolution(a,a) accurately.
- Objective S=sum(a). Score=2n/S^2.
- We want maximize S subject max c≤1, a_i≥0.
- Local search cycle:
  1. Saturation: cyclic coordinate ascent (increase each coord to feasible max) maybe multiple passes.
  2. Perturbation: choose a probability distribution over coordinates proportional to sensitivity to current peaks: d_i = Σ_{k∈active} w_k 2 a_{k-i}; multiply a by (1 - ε d_norm) to reduce sensitive coords. Also randomly reduce high a? Ensure a_i ≤1 (since c0 etc). Then saturation refills.
  3. Track best S/score.
This directly uses M=1 and avoids global scale. The score improvement is monotonically when S increases after saturation (max≤1). After perturbation S drops, but refill can exceed.

This could be more stable and likely improve hard target. Let's design in detail and compare with gradient.

Let's implement `refill_coordinates(a, c, passes=5, damp=0.5)`:
```
n=len(a); M=1.0
for _ in range(passes):
  improved=0
  for i in range(n):
    x=a[i]
    if x < 0: x=0
    seg=c[i:i+n]
    base = seg - 2*x*a
    base[i] = c[2*i] - x*x  # if 2i < L? always 2i up to 2n-2, i≤n-1 yes
    # For numerical base > M means infeasible; set bound 0
    lin_num = M - base
    # safe
    bounds = np.full(n, np.inf)
    positive = a > 0
    np.divide(lin_num, 2*a, out=bounds, where=positive)
    # For nonpositive a, leave inf; for negative base? if lin_num negative, bound negative; we will max 0
    # self:
    if lin_num[i] > 0: bounds[i]=np.sqrt(lin_num[i])
    else: bounds[i]=0.0
    y = float(np.min(bounds))
    if y < 0: y=0.0
    if y > 1.0: y=1.0 # since M=1
    if y > x*(1+1e-10):
       new = x + (y-x)*damp
       # ensure no c exceeds M? damp should keep feasible; but due min bound exact, any <=y feasible, so new<=y.
       delta = new-x
       # update c
       if delta !=0:
          c[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a
          c[2*i] = c[2*i] - 2*delta*x + new*new - x*x? Wait c[i:i+n] added 2δ a for all j including j=i. At j=i (k=2i), correct increment new^2 - x^2. We added 2δ x. Need subtract extra: c[2i] += (new^2 - x^2) - 2δ x = (new-x)(new+x) -2δ x = δ(new+x-2x)=δ(new-x)=δ^2. Since new^2-x^2 =2δx+δ^2. So c[2i] += δ^2. Nice.
          a[i]=new
          improved++
  if improved==0: break
```
Check c update: We do `c[i:i+n] += 2*delta*a` using old a[i]=x. Then at self index `c[2*i] += delta*delta`. Because added 2δx, need δ^2 extra. Good. But if a[i] has been updated, `a` in segment for j=i still old x until after update. Good. For other j, old a_j unaffected. After update, a[i]=new.
Feasibility: Since y is min of bounds using current c before update. For new ≤ y, all constraints remain ≤M. However c update for self adds δ^2; included in bound. Good. Numerical errors maybe small; add periodic recompute.

But the bound computation `bound = (M-base)/(2*a)` for linear constraints. If a_j very small, bound huge; okay. If base slightly >M due numeric, bound negative; min negative could set y=0, causing infeasible? But if current feasible, base should ≤M - positive contributions? For j where current x>0, base = c - 2x a_j ≤ M - something, so M-base positive. For self, base=c[2i]-x^2 ≤M - x? Actually c includes x^2. Good.

Time can be improved by early break if y-x tiny. Use vector operations avoid Python scalar per i? Loop over i. Each iter: allocate base `c[i:i+n]` view? If we do `base = c[i:i+n] - 2*x*a`, creates new array length n. 5582 allocations per pass; okay maybe. To reduce, use preallocated? Not necessary.

But `np.divide(lin_num, 2*a, out=bounds, where=positive)` requires `bounds` array length n. We can create `bounds = lin_num / (2*a + tiny)` then set where a==0 to inf; simpler but division by zero. Use:

```

```
After updates, c may drift; every pass or every 500 coords, recompute c via direct/FFT to clean. FFT convolution of a. Use `c=fft_conv(a,a)`. For coordinate update, c exact enough but drift. Recompute at start of each pass. Saturation then O(n^2) plus FFT.

Perturbation:
- After saturation, we have local max S for M=1 (no coordinate can increase individually). Need move basin. Use subgradient to reduce coordinates contributing most to active peaks.
  Compute q=c. Active weights top maybe all coefficients > M*(1-eps) or softmax. Since M=1, peaks near1. Let weights w over c: `w = np.exp((c-1)/temp)` with temp small (e.g.0.05) or topk uniform over top T. Then d_i = 2Σ w_k a_{k-i}. This similar gradient. Normalize d to have mean? Reduce a: `a *= 1 - decay * (d - d.mean())`? Since we want reduce high d. But must ensure a nonnegative and ≤1. Better multiplicative: `a *= np.exp(-strength * normalized_d)`, then clip to [0,1] and renormalize? Renormalizing scaling up may exceed M=1. We should not rescale up. Just decrease: `a = a * (1 - decay * r)` with r normalized nonnegative (e.g. r=(d-mean positive)). Since multiplying by <1 for all? To keep sum drop, if subtract from all maybe. We can do: `r = d - np.median(d); r = np.clip(r,0,None); r /= r.max()+eps`; `factor = 1 - decay*r`; `a *= factor`. This reduces sensitive coordinates more. All factors ≤1, so max c never increases? Reducing any a_i decreases all c_k (since nonnegative), so feasibility guaranteed. Good. Then refill increases sum.
- Strength decay schedule: start small 0.05, increase if no improvement? Or random.
- Also random perturbation: multiply a by `1 - 0.05*random` on random subset.

Implementation details for `coordinate_basin_search(a, deadline, max_actual_evals)`:
Input a nonnegative any scale.
1. Normalize to M=1: compute c=fft_conv(a,a), m=c.max(). If m<=0 return. `a = a / sqrt(m)`. Then c should max1. Sum S.
2. Initial score = 2n/(S*S). Evaluate actual scaled? Since internal scale M=1, values a_i can be up to1; for actual score, scaling arbitrary; scale to max500 for actual. Score same. We'll track actual.
3. Need c for coordinate updates. Recompute c = fft_conv(a,a) to clean.
4. Loop:
   a. Saturation: run `saturation_pass(a,c, max_passes=?)` until no improvement or time. Maybe 2-5 passes. After saturation, recompute c clean.
   b. Compute S, score. If improved, update best.
   c. If time and no improvement after several cycles, perturb.
   Perturb adaptively:
      Compute weights: if local max stuck, use current c. Since M=1, many peaks near1? Baseline has many exactly1; weights top maybe half. Use softmax temp = 0.02? To avoid all equal, pick top 1000 coefficients: `idx = np.argsort(c)[-top_refill:]`; weights uniform or by c. But if baseline first n coefficients all1, top 1000 random among them; reducing a_i will affect those. Good. Could use weights over active set: coefficients > 1 - 1e-6. Maybe many; direct gradient cost. Use top 200 with values. For simplicity, use `topk=200` uniform over top c. Compute d via direct? topk=200, n=5582 => 1.1M per perturb, cheap. Direct gradient:
```
d=np.zeros(n)
for k in top_indices:
    # d[i] += 2*w_k*a[k-i]
    # i range max(0,k-n+1) to min(n-1,k)
    lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
    # j=k-i; as i lo..hi, j=hi? For i=lo, j=k-lo; i=hi,j=k-hi. p_j reversed.
    # slice a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
    d[lo:hi+1] += 2*w * a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
```
   Normalize d: subtract median/mean and clip positive? For reducing, use `r = d - np.median(d); r = np.maximum(r,0); r /= r.sum()+eps; factor = 1 - decay*r*rsum?` If r sums maybe n*? We want coordinate reduction fraction up to decay. Use `a *= 1 - decay*r` where r normalized to max1? If r is distribution (sum1), decay*r small (max≤1/n) too small. Better `r = (d - np.median(d)); r = np.maximum(r,0); r = r/r.max()+eps`; factor = 1 - decay*r. Decay 0.05-0.2. This reduces high d coordinates by up to decay. Feasibility ok.
   Decay schedule: start 0.05; if S after refill not > best, increase decay up to 0.5? Or alternate.
   We can also more aggressively set a random subset to smaller: choose random coordinates with probability, multiply by 0.5.
5. Periodically evaluate actual best.

Let's implement coordinate method carefully with time checks.

Pseudo-code for coordinate method:
```
def refine_fixed_max(a, deadline, actual_eval_limit=20):
    a=np.maximum(a,0).astype(float)
    n=len(a)
    if a.sum()<=0: return None,inf,None,inf
    c=fft_conv(a,a); m=float(c.max())
    if m<=1e-300: return...
    a=a/np.sqrt(m)
    S=float(a.sum())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_S=S; best_score=2*n/(S*S)
    best_actual=None; best_actual_score=inf
    # evaluate initial actual after scaling to 500
    ev=scaled_for_eval(best_a); av=actual_score(ev); ...
    temp=0.1; no_improve=0
    sat_passes=2
   _iter=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       # saturate
       c=fft_conv(a,a); maxc=float(c.max())
       if maxc > 1+1e-8: a/=np.sqrt(maxc); S/=np.sqrt(maxc); c=fft_conv(a,a)
       for pass in range(sat_passes):
           if time > deadline: break
           improved=0
           for i in range(n):
               x=float(a[i])
               if x <= 0:
                   # If zero, feasible max maybe >0; compute bound too? yes even x=0, can increase.
               seg = c[i:i+n]  # length n
               if x == 0:
                   base = seg
               else:
                   base = seg - 2.0*x*a
               # set self index i (since i in 0..n-1)
               base_i = c[2*i] - x*x
               # Need base array with self updated. Instead of allocating base? We'll do.
               base[i] = base_i
               # bounds
               bounds = np.empty(n)
               pos = a > 1e-300
               # For all j, if pos: (1-base)/(2a), else inf
               np.divide(1.0 - base, 2.0*a, out=bounds, where=pos)
               bounds[~pos] = np.inf
               # self bound
               if base_i > 0:
                   bounds[i] = np.sqrt(base_i)
               else:
                   bounds[i] = 0.0
               # due numeric, if bound <0 set 0
               # y = min where finite
               y = float(np.min(bounds))
               if not np.isfinite(y) or y < 0: y = 0.0
               if y > 1.0: y = 1.0
               if y > x + 1e-12:
                   new = x + 0.5*(y-x)
                   delta = new - x
                   if delta > 0:
                       c[i:i+n] += 2.0*delta*a
                       c[2*i] += delta*delta
                       a[i] = new
                       improved += 1
           # after pass recompute c to avoid drift
           c = fft_conv(a,a)
           # ensure max<=1: if >1, scale a slightly
           maxc=c.max(); if maxc>1+1e-9: a/=np.sqrt(maxc+1e-12); c=fft_conv...
           if improved==0: break
       S=float(a.sum())
       score=2*n/(S*S)
       if score < best_score - 1e-9:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy(); no_improve=0
           # evaluate actual occasionally
           if actual_eval_count < limit and (iter%5==0 or no_improve>...): evaluate
       else:
           no_improve +=1
       # If no improvement for several, perturb
       if no_improve >= 2:
           c=fft_conv(a,a)
           # weights from top peaks (current max should 1)
           topk = min(n*2? 200)
           idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
           vals = c[idx]
           w = np.ones_like(vals)/topk  # or weighted by vals
           d = np.zeros(n)
           for kk in idx:
               lo=max(0,int(kk)-n+1); hi=min(n-1,int(kk))
               if hi>=lo:
                   # a[k-hi : k-lo+1] reversed
                   d[lo:hi+1] += 2* (1.0/topk) * a[int(kk)-hi:int(kk)-lo+1][::-1]
           r = d - np.median(d)
           r = np.maximum(r,0.0)
           if r.max()>0:
              r = r/r.max()
              a = a * (1.0 - 0.1*r)  # reducing
              a = np.clip(a,0,1.0)
              # after reduction, c decreases; no max issue.
           else:
              # random reduce
              mask=np.random.choice(n, n//10, replace=False); a[mask]*=0.8
           no_improve=0
       iter +=1
    final eval actual for best_a
    return best_a, best_score, best_actual_p, best_actual_score
```

Now, let's detail `local_search_gradient` with more robust line search and hyperparam schedule.

Since objective is homogeneous, we can fix sum=1. Use `p` probability.
Function signature: `def local_search_gradient(p0, deadline, rng, use_actual=True, max_actual=20):`
- p0: array nonnegative; normalize sum1.
- best_p_int, best_fast, best_actual.
- We will run phases with different topk/smooth: Instead of single loop, outer `smooth_phase` from [0.5,0.2,0.08,0.03,0.01, then anneal]. Each phase time slice. This focuses peaks more. Use momentum reset when phase changes.
- Inner iterations: compute q=fft_conv(p,p); maxq; score=2n maxq; weights: if topk mode? Use softmax with temp=smooth*maxq. For smooth small, top weight sharp.    - Update momentum for normalized gradient: `m=0.9*m+0.1*d`; `v=0.99*v+0.01*d*d`; `step = lr * m_hat / (sqrt(v_hat)+eps)`.

- When stuck (no improve 50), perturb: `p *= exp(0.05*noise)`; normalize; reset momentum; maybe change phase.

Detailed code skeleton:
```
"""
docstring...
"""
import numpy as np
import time
import math
try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
    _HAS_SCIPY_FFT=True
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft
    _HAS_SCIPY_FFT=False

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def _fft_conv(a,b):
   nout=len(a)+len(b)-1
   n=_next_pow2(nout)
   if _HAS_SCIPY_FFT:
      return irfft(rfft(a,n)*rfft(b,n), n)[:nout]
   else:
      return ifft(fft(a,n)*fft(b,n)).real[:nout]

def _fast_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=len(a)
   if n==0: return np.inf
   s=float(a.sum())
   if s <=1e-300: return np.inf
   p=a/s
   c=_fft_conv(p,p)
   mx=float(np.max(c))
   if mx < 0 and mx > -1e-12: mx=0.0
   return 2.0*n*mx

def _scale_for_eval(a, maxv=500.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   m=float(np.max(a)) if a.size else 0.0
   if m <= 1e-300:
      return np.full(a.shape, 1.0)
   out=a*(maxv/m)
   return np.clip(out,0.0,1000.0)

def _safe_actual(seq):
   try:
      val=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq])
   except Exception:
      return np.inf
   if val is None or not np.isfinite(val):
      return np.inf
   return float(val)
```
`_safe_actual` calls global evaluate_sequence. It's top-level, no closure. Good.
```

       p=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
       return None
    if p.ndim !=1 or p.size==0: return None
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    if p.sum()<=0: return None
    return p
```

Resample:
```
def _resample(arr, n):
   arr=np.asarray(arr,float)
   m=arr.size
   if m==n: return arr.copy()
   if m==1: return np.full(n, arr[0])
   x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   return np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
```

Central binomial:
```
def _central_binomial_even(n):
   b=np.empty(n)
   if n>0: b[0]=1.0
   for i in range(1,n): b[i]=b[i-1]*(2*i-1)/(2*i)
   return b
```

Candidate generation:
```
def _prepare_candidate(a, maxv=500.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
   if a.size==0: return None
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
   if float(a.sum())<=0: a=np.ones_like(a)
   m=float(a.max())
   if m<=0: a=np.ones_like(a)
   else: a=a*(maxv/m)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   return a

def _simple_candidates(n, parent, rng):
   cands=[]
   def add(name,a):
      b=_prepare_candidate(a)
      if b is not None:
         cands.append((name,b))
   # parent
   if parent is not None:
      add("parent", _resample(parent,n))
   k=np.arange(n,dtype=np.float64)
   t=k/(n-1) if n>1 else np.array([0.0])
   add("ones", np.ones(n))
   add("ramp", k)
   add("ramp_sq", k*k)
   add("inv_ramp", n-1-k)
   b=_central_binomial_even(n)
   add("binom", b)
   add("sym_binom", b + b[::-1])
   add("binom_ramp", b + 0.2*k)
   add("inv_sqrt", 1.0/np.sqrt(k+1))
   for p in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
       add(f"pow_{p}", np.power(k+1.0, -p) if p>0? Actually negative powers; also positive? two loops)
```

def local_search_gradient(p0, deadline, rng, max_actual=25):
    p=np.asarray(p0,float).copy()
```

Need `_corr_p_weights` top-level.
```
def _corr_p_weights(p,w):
   n=len(p); L=len(w)
   N=_next_pow2(n+L-1)
   pw=p[::-1]
   if _HAS: conv=irfft(rfft(pw,N)*rfft(w,N),N)[:n+L-1]
   else: ...
   idx=(L-1)-np.arange(n)
   return conv[idx]
```
Check: We want h_i=Σ_j p_j w_{i+j}. Using pw reversed? Let's verify with pw=p[::-1]. Convolution pw*w: conv[t]=Σ_s pw[s] w[t-s]=Σ_s p_{n-1-s} w[t-s]. Let j=n-1-s -> s=n-1-j. conv[t]=Σ_j p_j w[t - (n-1-j)] = Σ_j p_j w[j + t - n +1]. Want w[i+j] => set t - n +1 = i => t = i + n -1. So conv[i+n-1] = h_i. Previously using p (not reversed) and w_rev gave index. Let's test simple n=2, w length3. p=[p0,p1]. pw=[p1,p0]. conv[t]: t=1? i=0,n-1=1 => t=1. conv1=p1*w0? wait s0 p1*w1? let's compute: conv1 = sum_s pw[s]w[1-s]: s0 p1*w1, s1 p0*w0 => p1w1 + p0w0. But h0=p0w0+p1w1. ok. i=1 => t=2: conv2=p0*w2 + p1*w1? h1=p0w1+p1w2. Not match. Maybe different orientation.
Let's rederive. We want h_i = Σ_j p_j w_{i+j}.
Use convolution of p and reversed w: r[j]=w[L-1-j]. conv = p * r: conv[t]=Σ_j p_j r[t-j]=Σ_j p_j w[L-1-(t-j)]=Σ_j p_j w[j + L-1 -t]. Set L-1 - t = i => t=L-1-i. Good. So use `w_rev = w[::-1]`, conv[t], index L-1-i. That's previous. Implement:
```

conv = _fft_conv_len(p, wr, N?) but _fft_conv takes full lengths. Could use internal with N.
idx = (len(w)-1) - np.arange(len(p))
return conv[idx]
```
Test n=2,L=3. wr=[w2,w1,w0]. conv[2-i]: i=0 conv2=p0w0+p1w1; i=1 conv1=p0w1+p1w2. Good.
So `_corr_p_weights(p,w)`:
```
n=len(p); L=len(w); N=_next_pow2(n+L-1)
wr=w[::-1]
if scipy: f=rfft(p,N); g=rfft(wr,N); conv=irfft(f*g,N)
else: ...
return conv[(L-1) - np.arange(n)]
```
Works.

In coordinate search, we need c = `_fft_conv(a,a)` with length 2n-1.

Now coordinate search top-level. Let's write it with efficient bound using division but maybe okay. We'll include time checks.

```
def local_search_coordinate(a0, deadline, rng, max_actual=20):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a0,float),0)
    n=len(a)
    if a.size==0: return...
    c=_fft_conv(a,a); mx=float(np.max(c))
    if mx<=0: return...
    a=a/np.sqrt(mx)
    inv2a=np.full(n,np.inf)
    pos=a>1e-12
    inv2a[pos]=1.0/(2.0*a[pos])
    S=float(a.sum()); score=2*n/(S*S)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_S=S
    best_actual_p=..., best_actual_score=initial actual
    strength=0.05
    no=0
    it=0
    while time<deadline:
       # ensure max<=1
       c=_fft_conv(a,a); mx=float(np.max(c))
       if mx > 1.0 + 1e-8:
           a /= np.sqrt(mx*(1+1e-10)); c=_fft_conv(a,a); update inv2a
       # saturation: 2 passes
       sat_passes=min(3, max(1, int(2)))
       for ps in range(sat_passes):
           improved=0
           for i in range(n):
               if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
               x=float(a[i])
               # bounds
               bounds = np.full(n, np.inf)
               # linear terms
               # use c segment
               seg = c[i:i+n]
               # to avoid division by zero, use pos a
               if x != 0.0:
                   margin = 1.0 - (seg - 2.0*x*a)
               else:
                   margin = 1.0 - seg
               # for positive a:
               pos_a = a > 1e-12
               # valid where margin >= -tol
               tol=1e-9
               valid = pos_a & (margin >= -tol)
               if np.any(valid):
                   np.divide(np.maximum(margin,0.0), 2.0*a, out=bounds, where=valid)
               # if pos_a and margin < -tol -> infeasible; bounds=0
               bad = pos_a & (margin < -tol)
               if np.any(bad):
                   bounds[bad]=0.0
               # self
               self_margin = 1.0 - (c[2*i] - x*x)
               if self_margin >= -tol:
                   bounds[i]=math.sqrt(max(self_margin,0.0))
               else:
                   bounds[i]=0.0
               y=float(np.min(bounds))
               if not np.isfinite(y) or y<0: y=0.0
               if y>1.0: y=1.0
               if y > x + 1e-10:
                   new = x + 0.5*(y-x)
                   if new > 1.0: new=1.0
                   delta = new - x
                   if delta > 0:
                       c[i:i+n] += 2.0*delta*a
                       c[2*i] += delta*delta
                       a[i]=new
                       if new > 1e-12: inv2a[i]=1.0/(2.0*new)
                       improved +=1
           if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
           # clean c
           c=_fft_conv(a,a)
           mx=float(np.max(c))
           if mx > 1.0 + 1e-7:
               a/=np.sqrt(mx*(1+1e-9)); inv2a update all? Need update pos.
           if improved == 0:
               break
       S=float(a.sum()); score=2*n/(S*S)
       if score < best_score - 1e-8:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
           no=0
           # actual eval count limit
           if actual_count < max_actual:
              ev=_scale_for_eval(best_a); av=_safe_actual(ev); if av<best_actual...
              actual_count +=1
       else:
           no +=1
       if no >= 2:
           # perturb
           c=_fft_conv(a,a)
           topk_list=(30,80,200,500,1000)
           topk=topk_list[(it*7) % len(topk_list)]
           if topk > 2*n: topk=2*n-1
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
           d=np.zeros(n)
           # weight by values? uniform
           wq=(1.0/float(len(idx)))
           for kk in idx:
               k=int(kk)
               lo = k - (n-1)
               if lo < 0: lo=0
               hi = k
               if hi > n-1: hi=n-1
               if hi >= lo:
                   # slice a[k-hi : k-lo+1] reversed
                   d[lo:hi+1] += 2.0*wq*a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
           # reduce high d
           med=float(np.median(d))
           r=d-med
           r=np.maximum(r,0.0)
           rmax=float(np.max(r))
           if rmax > 1e-12:
               r=r/rmax
               a = a*(1.0 - strength*r)
               a=np.clip(a,0.0,1.0)
           else:
               mask=np.random.choice(n, max(1,n//20), replace=False)
               a[mask] *= 0.8
           # update inv2a maybe after perturbation: recompute
           inv2a=np.full(n,np.inf); pos=a>1e-12; inv2a[pos]=1/(2a[pos])
           # strength schedule
           if no > 6:
               strength = min(0.8, strength*1.3)
           else:
               strength = max(0.02, strength*0.9)
           no=0
       it +=1
    final eval best_a scaled
    return best_a,best_scorer,best_actual_p,best_actual_score
```
But inside loop using `np.random.choice`; ok. Need no nested.

We can do `margin = 1.0 - seg`; then if x>0 add `2*x*a`: `margin = margin + 2*x*a` creates. So one or two arrays. Could use precomputed inv but not. It's okay but maybe slow.

`_local_gradient_search`: We need to ensure `phase` / no variables. Let's write concrete:
```
def local_search_gradient(p0, deadline, rng, max_actual=20):
    p=...
    n=...
    cur=_fast_score(p)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_fast=cur
    best_actual_p=_scale_for_eval(p); best_actual_score=_safe_actual(best_actual_p)
    actual_count=1
    last_fast_for_actual=1e300
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
    lr=0.05
    smooth=0.5
    no=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q=_fft_conv(p,p)
        maxq=float(np.max(q))
        cur=2.0*n*maxq
        if cur < best_fast - 1e-10:
            best_fast=cur; best_p=p.copy()
        temp=smooth*maxq + 1e-18
        z=(q-maxq)/temp
        # to avoid all underflow when temp tiny: if z min < -700, clip
        w=np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0))
        ws=float(w.sum())
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            w=np.zeros_like(q)
            w[int(np.argmax(q))]=1.0
        else:
            w=w/ws
        grad=2.0*_corr_p_weights(p,w)
        grad=grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        rms=math.sqrt(float(np.mean(grad*grad)))
        if rms > 1e-300:
            grad=grad/rms
        else:
            grad=0
        m=0.85*m + 0.15*grad
        v=0.95*v + 0.05*grad*grad
        mhat=m/(np.sqrt(v)+1e-8)
        mrms=math.sqrt(float(np.mean(mhat*mhat)))
        if mrms > 1e-300:
            mhat=mhat/mrms
        else:
            mhat=0.0
        accepted=False
        for factor in (1.0,0.5,0.25,0.1):
            step=lr*factor*mhat
            if float(np.max(np.abs(step))) <=1e-12:
                continue
            step=np.clip(step,-5.0,5.0)
            cand=p*np.exp(-step)
            cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0)
            cs=float(cand.sum())
            if cs <=0: continue
            cand=cand/cs
            cfast=float(_fast_score(cand))
            if cfast < cur - 1e-12:
                p=cand; cur=cfast; accepted=True; lr=min(0.5, lr*1.05); break
        if not accepted:
            proj=grad - float(np.mean(grad))
            for factor in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
                eta=lr*factor
                if eta <=1e-6: break
                cand=p - eta*proj
                cand=np.maximum(cand,0.0); cs=float(cand.sum())
                if cs <=0: continue
                cand=cand/cs
                cfast=float(_fast_score(cand))
                if cfast < cur - 1e-12:
                    p=cand; cur=cfast; accepted=True; lr=min(0.5, lr*1.05); break
        if not accepted:
            no += 1
            if no > 20:
                eps=0.03 + 0.01*min(no,50)
                p=p*np.exp(eps*rng.standard_normal(n))
                p=np.maximum(p,1e-300)
                cs=float(p.sum())
                if cs>0: p=p/cs
                m*=0.5; v*=0.5; lr=0.05
                smooth=max(0.003, smooth*0.5)
                no=0
            else:
                lr=max(0.002, lr*0.8)
        # adjust smooth periodically: shrink
        if it % 1000 == 0:
            smooth *= 0.7
            if smooth < 0.002:
                smooth = 0.1
        # actual eval
        if actual_count < max_actual and (it % 800 == 0 or best_fast < last_fast_for_actual - 1e-6):
            ev=_scale_for_eval(p)
            av=_safe_actual(ev)
            if av < best_actual_score:
                best_actual_score=av; best_actual_p=ev.copy()
            actual_count +=1
            last_fast_for_actual=best_fast
        it +=1
    # final evaluate best_p
    ev=_scale_for_eval(best_p); av=_safe_actual(ev)
    if av < best_actual_score: best_actual_score=av; best_actual_p=ev.copy()
    return best_p,best_fast,best_actual_p,best_actual_score
```

```
def _local_coordinate_search(a0, deadline, rng, max_actual=15):
   a=...
   c=_fft_conv(a,a); mx=...
   if mx<=0: return None...
   a=a/math.sqrt(mx)
   inv2a=...
   S=...
   score=...
   best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score; best_S=S
   best_actual_p=_scale_for_eval(best_a); best_actual_score=_safe_actual(best_actual_p); actual_count=1
   strength=0.1
   no=0; it=0
   while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       c=_fft_conv(a,a)
       mx=float(np.max(c))
       if mx > 1.0 + 1e-8:
           a = a / math.sqrt(mx*(1.0+1e-9))
           inv2a=np.full(len(a),np.inf); pos=a>1e-12; inv2a[pos]=1/(2*a[pos])
           c=_fft_conv(a,a)
       sat_passes=2 if len(a)<=6000 else 1
       for ps in range(sat_passes):
           improved=0
           for i in range(n):
               if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
               x=float(a[i])
               bounds=np.full(n, np.inf)
               segment = c[i:i+n]
               if x != 0.0:
                   margin = 1.0 - segment + 2.0*x*a
               else:
                   margin = 1.0 - segment
               tol=1e-9
               pos_a = a > 1e-12
               valid = pos_a & (margin >= -tol)
               if np.any(valid):
                   # use np.divide with out
                   np.divide(np.maximum(margin,0.0), 2.0*a, out=bounds, where=valid)
               bad = pos_a & (margin < -tol)
               if np.any(bad):
                   bounds[bad] = 0.0
               self_margin = 1.0 - (c[2*i] - x*x)
               if self_margin >= -tol:
                   bounds[i] = math.sqrt(max(self_margin, 0.0))
               else:
                   bounds[i] = 0.0
               y=float(np.min(bounds))
               if not np.isfinite(y) or y < 0.0:
                   y=0.0
               y=min(y,1.0)
               if y > x + 1e-10:
                   new_val = x + 0.5*(y-x)
                   if new_val > 1.0: new_val=1.0
                   delta = new_val - x
                   if delta > 0.0:
                       segment_update = 2.0*delta*a
                       c[i:i+n] = c[i:i+n] + segment_update
                       c[2*i] += delta*delta
                       a[i]=new_val
                       if new_val > 1e-12:
                           inv2a[i]=1.0/(2.0*new_val)
                       improved += 1
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           c=_fft_conv(a,a)
           mx=float(np.max(c))
           if mx > 1.0 + 1e-7:
               factor=math.sqrt(mx*(1.0+1e-9))
               a=a/factor
               inv2a=np.full(n,np.inf); pos=a>1e-12; inv2a[pos]=1.0/(2.0*a[pos])
               c=_fft_conv(a,a)
           if improved == 0:
               break
       S=float(a.sum()); score=2.0*n/(S*S)
       if score < best_score - 1e-8:
           best_score=score; best_a=a.copy()
           no=0
           if actual_count < max_actual:
               ev=_scale_for_eval(best_a); av=_safe_actual(ev)
               if av < best_actual_score:
                   best_actual_score=av; best_actual_p=ev.copy()
               actual_count +=1
       else:
           no += 1
       if no >= 2:
           c=_fft_conv(a,a)
           L=len(c)
           topks=(30,100,300,1000)
           topk=topks[(it*3) % len(topks)]
           if topk > L-1: topk=L-1
           if topk < 1: topk=1
           idx=np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
           d=np.zeros(n)
           wq=2.0/float(len(idx))
           for kk in idx:
               k=int(kk)
               lo=k-(n-1)
               if lo < 0: lo=0
               hi=k
               if hi > n-1: hi=n-1
               if hi >= lo:
                   d[lo:hi+1] += wq*a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
           med=float(np.median(d))
           r=d-med
           r=np.maximum(r,0.0)
           rmax=float(np.max(r))
           if rmax > 1e-12:
               r=r/rmax
               a=a*(1.0 - strength*r)
               a=np.clip(a,0.0,1.0)
               # ensure not all zero
               if float(a.sum()) <= 0:
                   a=best_a.copy()
           else:
               cnt=max(1,n//20)
               mask=rng.integers(0,n,cnt)
               a[mask] *= 0.7
           inv2a=np.full(n,np.inf); pos=a>1e-12; inv2a[pos]=1.0/(2.0*a[pos])
           if no > 6:
               strength=min(0.8, strength*1.25)
           else:
               strength=max(0.03, strength*0.9)
           no=0
       it += 1
   ev=_scale_for_eval(best_a); av=_safe_actual(ev)
   if av < best_actual_score: ...
   return best_a,best_score,best_actual_p,best_actual_score
```

In coordinate saturation, if x=0, `margin=1-segment`. For pos_a & margin>=-tol, divide margin by 2a. For a_j with valid and margin=0, bound zero. Good. If margin >0, bound positive. For a_j=0, bounds remain inf. For self, if x=0 and c[2i]=0, bound sqrt(1)=1. Good.

If a_i already >0 and c[2i]-x^2 + x^2 = c[2i]; self bound sqrt(1-c[2i])? If current c[2i]=1 and x small, self bound sqrt(x²)=x, so cannot increase via self. Good.

Linear bounds using margin =1-c_segment+2x a_j =1-(c_segment-2x a_j). If new x', constraint: margin + 2x'a_j ≤1 -> x' ≤ (1-margin)/(2a_j) = (c_segment-2x a_j? Wait margin =1 - current_without_x? For current feasible, margin≥0. If we set new≤min, ok.

```python
"""
Adaptive sequence constructor for minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over nonnegative sequences.

The program:
- evaluates a library of useful nonnegative templates (parent, polynomial-square-root,
  powers, Gaussians, smooth ramps, etc.) using the real evaluator when possible,
- mixes the best templates by linear interpolation,
- runs a mirror/Adam-style local search on the probability simplex with FFT-based
  gradient estimates of the peak convolution coefficient,
- then runs a fixed-maximum coordinate basin-hopping search that maximizes the sum
  while keeping max convolution bounded, which directly lowers the normalized objective.

The final returned sequence is the best sequence found according to the actual
evaluate_sequence() call.
"""

import time
import math

import numpy as np

try:
    from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft
    _HAS_SCIPY_FFT = True
except Exception:
    from numpy.fft import fft, ifft
    _HAS_SCIPY_FFT = False


def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (int(m) - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_conv(a, b):
    nout = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    n = _next_pow2(nout)
    if _HAS_SCIPY_FFT:
        fa = rfft(a, n)
        fb = rfft(b, n)
        out = irfft(fa * fb, n)
    else:
        fa = fft(a, n)
        fb = fft(b, n)
        out = ifft(fa * fb).real
    return out[:nout]


def _fast_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.inf
    p = a / s
    c = _fft_conv(p, p)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if mx < 0.0:
        if mx > -1e-11:
            mx = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mx)


def _scale_for_eval(a, maxv=500.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(a))
    if m <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(a.shape, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    out = a * (maxv / m)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0).astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _safe_actual(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in seq])
    except NameError:
        val = _fast_score(np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64))
    except Exception:
        val = np.inf
    if val is None:
        return np.inf
    try:
        val = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return np.inf
    return val


def _get_parent_sequence():
    try:
        p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(np.sum(p)) <= 0.0:
        return None
    return p


def _resample(arr, n):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    m = arr.size
    if m == n:
        return arr.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)


def _prepare_candidate(a, maxv=500.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
    m = float(np.max(a))
    if m <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones_like(a)
    else:
        a = a * (maxv / m)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    return a.astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def _try_append(cands, name, a):
    b = _prepare_candidate(a)
    if b is not None:
        cands.append((name, b))


def _central_binomial_even(n):
    b = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return b
    b[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(1, n):
        b[i] = b[i - 1] * (2 * i - 1) / (2 * i)
    return b


def _simple_candidates(n, parent, rng):
    cands = []
    k = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n > 1:
        t = k / float(n - 1)
    else:
        t = np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        _try_append(cands, "parent", _resample(parent, n))

    _try_append(cands, "ones", np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    b = _central_binomial_even(n)
    _try_append(cands, "binom_sqrt", b)
    _try_append(cands, "sym_binom_sqrt", b + b[::-1])
    _try_append(cands, "binom_plus_ramp", b + 0.05 * k)
    _try_append(cands, "inv_sqrt", 1.0 / np.sqrt(k + 1.0))

    # One-sided power shapes, around the square-root singularity and some smoother ones.
    for ex in (-0.75, -0.6, -0.5, -0.4, -0.25):
        _try_append(cands, "power_neg_%s" % ex, np.power(k + 1.0, ex))

    for ex in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
        _try_append(cands, "power_pos_%s" % ex, np.power(k + 1.0, ex))

    # Smooth ramps and endpoint Gaussians.
    _try_append(cands, "sin_half", np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * t))
    _try_append(cands, "sin_half_sq", np.sin(0.5 * np.pi * t) ** 2)
    _try_append(cands, "ramp_linear", k)
    _try_append(cands, "ramp_quad", k * k)
    _try_append(cands, "ramp_inv", n - 1.0 - k)

    for wdt in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
        _try_append(cands, "edge_gauss_%s" % wdt, np.exp(-(t * t) / wdt))

    for wdt in (0.02, 0.05, 0.10):
        _try_append(cands, "center_gauss_%s" % wdt, np.exp(-((t - 0.5) * (t - 0.5)) / wdt))

    _try_append(cands, "u_shape1", 1.0 + np.abs(t - 0.5))
    _try_append(cands, "u_shape2", 1.0 + (2.0 * t - 1.0) * (2.0 * t - 1.0))

    # A little controlled randomness, but keep it deterministic per seed.
    _try_append(cands, "random_lognormal", np.exp(0.45 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)))

    if len(cands) > 30:
        cands = cands[:30]
    return cands


def _corr_p_w(p, w):
    """
    Return d_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}, for i=0..len(p)-1.
    This is the raw unnormalized gradient factor for peak-weighted convolution.
    """
    n = len(p)
    L = len(w)
    N = _next_pow2(n + L - 1)
    wr = w[::-1]
    if _HAS_SCIPY_FFT:
        fp = rfft(p, N)
        fw = rfft(wr, N)
        conv = irfft(fp * fw, N)[:n + L - 1]
    else:
        fp = fft(p, N)
        fw = fft(wr, N)
        conv = ifft(fp * fw).real[:n + L - 1]
    idx = (L - 1) - np.arange(n)
    return conv[idx]


def local_search_gradient(a0, deadline, rng, max_actual=6):
    p = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None, np.inf, None, np.inf

    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        p = np.ones_like(p)
        s = float(p.size)
    p = p / s
    n = len(p)

    cur = _fast_score(p)
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_fast = cur

    best_actual_p = _scale_for_eval(p)
    best_actual_score = _safe_actual(best_actual_p)
    actual_count = 1
    last_fast_for_actual = best_fast

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    lr = 0.1
    smooth = 0.5
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        q = _fft_conv(p, p)
        maxq = float(np.max(q))
        cur = float(2.0 * n * maxq)

        if cur < best_fast - 1e-10:
            best_fast = cur
            best_p = p.copy()

        temp = smooth * maxq + 1e-18
        z = (q - maxq) / temp
        w = np.exp(np.clip(z, -700.0, 0.0))
        ws = float(np.sum(w))
        if ws <= 1e-300:
            w = np.zeros_like(q)
            w[int(np.argmax(q))] = 1.0
        else:
            w = w / ws

        grad = 2.0 * _corr_p_w(p, w)
        grad = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
        rms = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(grad * grad)))
        if rms > 1e-300:
            grad = grad / rms
        else:
            grad = np.zeros_like(grad)

        m = 0.85 * m + 0.15 * grad
        v = 0.95 * v + 0.05 * (grad * grad)
        mhat = m / (np.sqrt(v) + 1e-8)
        mrms = math.sqrt(float(np.mean(mhat * mhat)))
        if mrms > 1e-300:
            mhat = mhat / mrms
        else:
            mhat = np.zeros_like(mhat)

        accepted = False

        # Mirror / multiplicative steps.
        for factor in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.1):
            step = lr * factor * mhat
            if float(np.max(np.abs(step))) <= 1e-12:
                continue
            step = np.clip(step, -5.0, 5.0)
            cand = p * np.exp(-step)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, 1e-300)
            cs = float(np.sum(cand))
            if cs <= 1e-300:
                continue
            cand = cand / cs
            cfast = _fast_score(cand)
            if cfast < cur - 1e-12:
                p = cand
                cur = cfast
                accepted = True
                lr = min(0.5, lr * 1.05)
                break

        # Euclidean projected-like fallback.
        if not accepted:
            proj = grad - float(np.mean(grad))
            for factor in (0.5, 0.25, 0.1, 0.05):
                eta = lr * factor
                if eta <= 1e-6:
                    break
                cand = p - eta * proj
                cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                cs = float(np.sum(cand))
                if cs <= 1e-300:
                    continue
                cand = cand / cs
                cfast = _fast_score(cand)
                if cfast < cur - 1e-12:
                    p = cand
                    cur = cfast
                    accepted = True
                    lr = min(0.5, lr * 1.05)
                    break

        if not accepted:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 20:
                eps = 0.03 + 0.01 * min(no_improve, 50)
                p = p * np.exp(eps * rng.standard_normal(n))
                p = np.maximum(p, 1e-300)
                cs = float(np.sum(p))
                if cs > 1e-300:
                    p = p / cs
                m *= 0.5
                v *= 0.5
                lr = 0.1
                smooth = max(0.003, smooth * 0.5)
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.8)

        if it % 1000 == 0:
            smooth *= 0.7
            if smooth < 0.002:
                smooth = 0.1

        if actual_count < max_actual and (
            (it > 0 and it % 1500 == 0) or
            (best_fast < last_fast_for_actual - 1e-6)
        ):
            ev = _scale_for_eval(p)
            av = _safe_actual(ev)
            if av < best_actual_score:
                best_actual_score = av
                best_actual_p = ev.copy()
            actual_count += 1
            last_fast_for_actual = best_fast

        it += 1

    ev = _scale_for_eval(best_p)
    av = _safe_actual(ev)
    if av < best_actual_score:
        best_actual_score = av
        best_actual_p = ev.copy()

    return best_p, best_fast, best_actual_p, best_actual_score


def local_search_coordinate(a0, deadline, rng, max_actual=5):
    """
    Coordinate search with max(a*a) fixed to 1 and sum maximized.
    The score is 2*n / sum^2 when max(a*a)=1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None, np.inf, None, np.inf

    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
        a = np.ones_like(a)

    c = _fft_conv(a, a)
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if mx <= 1e-300:
        return None, np.inf, None, np.inf

    a = a / math.sqrt(mx)
    n = len(a)

    inv2a = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
    pos = a > 1e-12
    inv2a[pos] = 1.0 / (2.0 * a[pos])

    S = float(np.sum(a))
    score = float(2.0 * n / (S * S))

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score

    best_actual_p = _scale_for_eval(best_a)
    best_actual_score = _safe_actual(best_actual_p)
    actual_count = 1

    strength = 0.1
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _fft_conv(a, a)
        mx = float(np.max(c))
        if mx > 1.0 + 1e-8:
            a = a / math.sqrt(mx * (1.0 + 1e-9))
            inv2a = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
            pos = a > 1e-12
            inv2a[pos] = 1.0 / (2.0 * a[pos])
            c = _fft_conv(a, a)

        sat_passes = 2 if n <= 6000 else 1
        for _ in range(sat_passes):
            improved = 0
            for i in range(n):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break

                x = float(a[i])
                bounds = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
                segment = c[i:i + n]

                if x != 0.0:
                    margin = 1.0 - segment + 2.0 * x * a
                else:
                    margin = 1.0 - segment

                tol = 1e-9
                pos_a = a > 1e-12
                valid = pos_a & (margin >= -tol)

                if np.any(valid):
                    np.divide(np.maximum(margin, 0.0), 2.0 * a, out=bounds, where=valid)

                bad = pos_a & (margin < -tol)
                if np.any(bad):
                    bounds[bad] = 0.0

                self_margin = 1.0 - (c[2 * i] - x * x)
                if self_margin >= -tol:
                    bounds[i] = math.sqrt(max(self_margin, 0.0))
                else:
                    bounds[i] = 0.0

                y = float(np.min(bounds))
                if not np.isfinite(y) or y < 0.0:
                    y = 0.0
                if y > 1.0:
                    y = 1.0

                if y > x + 1e-10:
                    new_val = x + 0.5 * (y - x)
                    if new_val > 1.0:
                        new_val = 1.0
                    delta = new_val - x
                    if delta > 0.0:
                        c[i:i + n] += 2.0 * delta * a
                        c[2 * i] += delta * delta
                        a[i] = new_val
                        if new_val > 1e-12:
                            inv2a[i] = 1.0 / (2.0 * new_val)
                        improved += 1

            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break

            c = _fft_conv(a, a)
            mx = float(np.max(c))
            if mx > 1.0 + 1e-7:
                factor = math.sqrt(mx * (1.0 + 1e-9))
                a = a / factor
                inv2a = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
                pos = a > 1e-12
                inv2a[pos] = 1.0 / (2.0 * a[pos])
                c = _fft_conv(a, a)

            if improved == 0:
                break

        S = float(np.sum(a))
        if S > 0.0:
            score = float(2.0 * n / (S * S))

        if score < best_score - 1e-8:
            best_score = score
            best_a = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0

            if actual_count < max_actual:
                ev = _scale_for_eval(best_a)
                av = _safe_actual(ev)
                if av < best_actual_score:
                    best_actual_score = av
                    best_actual_p = ev.copy()
                actual_count += 1
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if no_improve >= 2:
            c = _fft_conv(a, a)
            L = len(c)
            topks = (30, 100, 300, 1000)
            topk = topks[(it * 3) % len(topks)]
            if topk > L - 1:
                topk = L - 1
            if topk < 1:
                topk = 1

            idx = np.argpartition(c, -topk)[-topk:]
            d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            wq = 2.0 / float(len(idx))

            for kk in idx:
                k = int(kk)
                lo = k - (n - 1)
                if lo < 0:
                    lo = 0
                hi = k
                if hi > n - 1:
                    hi = n - 1
                if hi >= lo:
                    d[lo:hi + 1] += wq * a[k - hi:k - lo + 1][::-1]

            med = float(np.median(d))
            r = d - med
            r = np.maximum(r, 0.0)
            rmax = float(np.max(r))

            if rmax > 1e-12:
                r = r / rmax
                a = a * (1.0 - strength * r)
                a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1.0)
                if float(np.sum(a)) <= 0.0:
                    a = best_a.copy()
            else:
                cnt = max(1, n // 20)
                mask = rng.integers(0, n, cnt)
                a[mask] *= 0.7

            inv2a = np.full(n, np.inf, dtype=np.float64)
            pos = a > 1e-12
            inv2a[pos] = 1.0 / (2.0 * a[pos])

            if no_improve > 6:
                strength = min(0.8, strength * 1.25)
            else:
                strength = max(0.03, strength * 0.9)
            no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    ev = _scale_for_eval(best_a)
    av = _safe_actual(ev)
    if av < best_actual_score:
        best_actual_score = av
        best_actual_p = ev.copy()

    return best_a, best_score, best_actual_p, best_actual_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 30.0)
    deadline = t0 + budget

    parent = _get_parent_sequence()

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        if parent.size <= 8192:
            n = int(parent.size)
        else:
            n = 8192
    else:
        n = 8192

    if n < 500:
        n = 4096

    print("Starting search, n=%d, budget=%.1fs" % (n, budget), flush=True)

    cands = _simple_candidates(n, parent, rng)
    top = []
    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf

    for name, a in cands:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        sc = _safe_actual(a)
        top.append((float(sc), a.copy()))
        if sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
        print("candidate %-24s score=%.6f" % (name, sc), flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _prepare_candidate(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
            best_score = _safe_actual(best_seq)

    if top:
        scores = np.array([x[0] for x in top], dtype=np.float64)
        order = np.argsort(scores)
    else:
        order = np.array([], dtype=int)

    top_sorted = []
    for i in order:
        top_sorted.append(top[int(i)])

    # Mix the best simple templates and test the most promising mixes.
    if len(top_sorted) >= 2 and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        m = min(3, len(top_sorted))
        mixes = []
        for ii in range(m):
            for jj in range(ii + 1, m):
                u = top_sorted[ii][1]
                v = top_sorted[jj][1]
                for num in range(1, 10):
                    tt = float(num) / 10.0
                    a = (1.0 - tt) * u + tt * v
                    b = _prepare_candidate(a)
                    if b is not None:
                        fsc = _fast_score(b)
                        mixes.append((float(fsc), b))

        if mixes:
            mscores = np.array([x[0] for x in mixes], dtype=np.float64)
            morder = np.argsort(mscores)
            tested = 0
            for idx in morder:
                if tested >= 8:
                    break
                if time.monotonic() > deadline - 90.0:
                    break
                fsc, b = mixes[int(idx)]
                sc = _safe_actual(b)
                top.append((float(sc), b.copy()))
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_seq = b.copy()
                print("mix fast=%.6f actual=%.6f" % (fsc, sc), flush=True)
                tested += 1

    # Re-sort after mixes if there are more than a few.
    if len(top) > 5:
        scores = np.array([x[0] for x in top], dtype=np.float64)
        order = np.argsort(scores)
        top_sorted = [top[int(i)] for i in order]

    # Gradient local search from the best simple/mixed candidates.
    seeds = []
    if best_seq is not None:
        seeds.append(best_seq.copy())
    for i in range(1, min(3, len(top_sorted))):
        seeds.append(top_sorted[i][1].copy())

    local_start = time.monotonic()
    local_end = deadline - 25.0
    if local_end > local_start + 90.0 and seeds:
        if len(seeds) == 1:
            fracs = [0.95]
        elif len(seeds) == 2:
            fracs = [0.6, 0.4]
        else:
            fracs = [0.5, 0.3, 0.2]

        total_local = local_end - local_start
        print("Gradient local search: %d seeds, %.1fs total" % (len(seeds), total_local), flush=True)

        for idx, sd in enumerate(seeds):
            rem = local_end - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 60.0:
                break
            alloc = total_local * fracs[idx]
            sd_deadline = min(local_end, time.monotonic() + max(60.0, alloc))
            gp, gfast, gap, gascore = local_search_gradient(
                sd, sd_deadline, rng, max_actual=5
            )
            if gascore < best_score:
                best_score = gascore
                best_seq = gap.copy()
            print(
                "gradient seed %d fast=%.6f actual=%.6f best=%.6f"
                % (idx + 1, gfast, gascore, best_score),
                flush=True
            )

    # Fixed-max coordinate basin hopping on the current best actual sequence.
    if best_seq is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 120.0:
        coord_start = time.monotonic()
        coord_end = min(
            deadline - 15.0,
            coord_start + max(120.0, 0.75 * (deadline - 15.0 - coord_start))
        )
        print(
            "Coordinate fixed-max search: budget %.1fs" % (coord_end - coord_start),
            flush=True
        )
        ca, cscore, cp, csactual = local_search_coordinate(
            best_seq, coord_end, rng, max_actual=5
        )
        if csactual < best_score:
            best_score = csactual
            best_seq = cp.copy()
        print(
            "coordinate score=%.6f best=%.6f" % (cscore, best_score),
            flush=True
        )

    # Final sanity / evaluation.
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = _prepare_candidate(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))
    else:
        best_seq = _prepare_candidate(best_seq)

    final_score = _safe_actual(best_seq)
    if final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score

    print("Final returned score: %.10f" % best_score, flush=True)
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
